## 10. Order

Two shapes: **OrderListItem** (lists) and **OrderView** (detail, and the response of every order action). Both are produced by `services/orderQueries.js`; the app **renders actions only from `allowed_actions` / `dispute_reasons` of OrderView** (never from `status`/`payment_status`).

### 10.1 OrderListItem — `GET /shop/orders` (buyer) and `GET /shop/orders/shop` (seller) → `data.orders[]`; also the rows of `GET /shop/checkouts/:id`

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | `int` | no | |
| `order_number` | `orderNumber` | `String` | no | shown to users and sent as UPI note |
| `checkout_id` | `checkoutId` | `int?` | yes | group of orders from one checkout |
| `shop_id` | `shopId` | `int` | no | |
| `shop_name` | `shopName` | `String` | buyer list & checkout rows | absent in seller list |
| `customer_username` | `customerUsername` | `String?` | seller list only | |
| `status` | `status` | `OrderStatus` | no | §15 |
| `payment_status` | `paymentStatus` | `PaymentStatus` | no | §15 |
| `payment_method` | `paymentMethod` | `PaymentMethod` | no | `upi`\|`cod` |
| `total_paise` | `totalPaise` | `int` | no | |
| `created_at`, `updated_at` | | `DateTime` | no | |
| `pay_by` | `payBy` | `DateTime?` | yes | UPI orders only |
| `confirm_by` | `confirmBy` | `DateTime?` | yes | set when the buyer claims |
| `pay_seconds_left` | `paySecondsLeft` | `int?` | yes | in list rows it is **raw** `TIMESTAMPDIFF` (can be ≤ 0 or null for COD); use it only with `payment_status == awaiting_payment` |
| `needs_action` | `needsAction` | `bool` | no | list endpoints only (buyer: awaiting payment and time left; seller: claimed / refund_due / fulfilment pending). Real boolean |
| `items` | `items` | `List<OrderItem>` | no | attached to buyer/seller list rows; **not** attached to `GET /shop/checkouts/:id` rows |

Rows of the **checkout confirmation** `GET /shop/checkouts/:id` → `data.checkout = { id, created_at, orders: [OrderListItem without items, needs_action] }` (the same `LIST_COLUMNS` as above plus `shop_name`).

### 10.2 OrderView — `GET /shop/orders/:id`, and `data.order` of claim/edit-claim/confirm/not-received/refund/dispute/status/cancel

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id`, `order_number`, `checkout_id`, `shop_id`, `shop_name` | | | | as 10.1 (`checkout_id` nullable) |
| `shop_status` | `shopStatus` | `ShopStatus` | no | |
| `customer_user_id` | `customerUserId` | `int` | no | |
| `customer_username` | `customerUsername` | `String?` | yes | **omitted for the buyer** (`undefined`); present for seller/admin |
| `owner_user_id` | `ownerUserId` | `int` | no | the shop owner |
| `status` | `status` | `OrderStatus` | no | |
| `payment_status` | `paymentStatus` | `PaymentStatus` | no | |
| `payment_method` | `paymentMethod` | `PaymentMethod` | no | |
| `subtotal_paise`, `shipping_paise`, `total_paise` | | `int` | no | `shipping_paise` is currently always 0 (verified) |
| `notes` | `notes` | `String?` | yes | buyer's note, ≤ 500 chars |
| `created_at`, `updated_at` | | `DateTime` | no | |
| `pay_by` | `payBy` | `DateTime?` | yes | |
| `pay_seconds_left` | `paySecondsLeft` | `int?` | yes | **only when `payment_status == awaiting_payment`, else `null`**. May be negative during the grace window (see doc 06) |
| `confirm_by` | `confirmBy` | `DateTime?` | yes | |
| `confirm_seconds_left` | `confirmSecondsLeft` | `int?` | yes | **only when `claimed`, else `null`** |
| `confirmed_at` | `confirmedAt` | `DateTime?` | yes | |
| `cancelled_at` | `cancelledAt` | `DateTime?` | yes | |
| `cancelled_by` | `cancelledBy` | `CancelledBy?` | yes | `buyer`\|`seller`\|`system`\|`admin` |
| `cancel_reason` | `cancelReason` | `String?` | yes | |
| `full_name` | `shipFullName` | `String?` | yes | delivery address **copied onto the order** (keys are unprefixed in the view: `full_name, phone, line1, line2, city, state, pincode`) |
| `phone`, `line1`, `line2`, `city`, `state`, `pincode` | `shipPhone`, … | `String?` | yes | nullable for legacy orders |
| `items` | `items` | `List<OrderItem>` | no | §10.3 |
| `claim` | `claim` | `Claim?` | yes | §11.1 |
| `disputes` | `disputes` | `List<Dispute>` | no | §11.2, oldest first, usually 0–1 open |
| `viewer_role` | `viewerRole` | `ViewerRole` | no | `buyer`\|`seller`\|`admin` |
| `allowed_actions` | `allowedActions` | `List<String>` | no | vocabulary in §15.6 |
| `dispute_reasons` | `disputeReasons` | `List<DisputeReason>` | no | reasons the viewer may open **now** |
| `chat_read_only` | `chatReadOnly` | `bool` | no | |
| `payee` | `payee` | `Payee?` | yes | **only for the buyer while `awaiting_payment` and the shop is approved**, otherwise `null` |

**Keys that exist in the database row but are NOT in the view** (never expect them): `payee_upi_id`, `payee_name` (use `payee`), `address_id`, `stock_released_at`, `customer_*` internals, `pay_open`, `confirm_overdue`, `not_delivered_eligible`, `paid_after_cancel_open`, `stale`.

`Payee`: `{ upi_id: String, name: String?, upi_link: String? }` — `upi_link` is a ready `upi://pay?pa=…&pn=…&am=<rupees.paise>&tn=<order_number>&cu=INR` (null if the stored UPI ID fails validation). The app should use `upi_link` as given rather than rebuilding it.

### 10.3 OrderItem (`items[]`, `SELECT *` from `shop_order_items`)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | `int` | no | |
| `order_id` | `orderId` | `int` | no | |
| `product_id` | `productId` | `int` | no | |
| `product_title_snapshot` | `title` | `String` | no | title at purchase time |
| `unit_price_paise` | `unitPricePaise` | `int` | no | |
| `quantity` | `quantity` | `int` | no | |
| `line_total_paise` | `lineTotalPaise` | `int` | no | |

No image is stored on order items; resolve a thumbnail from the product only if it still exists (optional).

### 10.4 Checkout request and result

`POST /shop/checkout` (header `Idempotency-Key`, 8–64 chars `[\w-]`; missing/invalid → 400 `A valid Idempotency-Key header (8-64 letters, digits, - or _) is required`) body:

| JSON key | Dart source | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `address_id` | `int` | required |
| `items` | `[{product_id:int, quantity:int}]` | 1–50 lines, quantity 1–99; duplicate products are merged server-side |
| `payment_methods` | `{ "<shopId>": "upi"\|"cod" }` | **one entry per shop in the cart is required** (keys are shop ids as strings); a missing entry → 400 `Choose UPI or cash on delivery for "<shop name>"`. A legacy single `payment_method` applies to every shop |
| `notes` | `String?` | ≤ 500 |

Response `201` (or `200` when replayed with the same key and same cart): `data = { checkout_id: int, replayed: bool, orders: [CheckoutOrder] }`, where `CheckoutOrder = { id, order_number, shop_id, shop_name, total_paise, status, payment_status, payment_method, pay_by }` (no items, no seconds-left).

### 10.5 Other order request bodies (field names only; endpoints/errors in doc 05)

| Action | Body |
|---|---|
| claim | `{ utr, screenshot? }` (`screenshot` = data URL, stored privately) |
| edit claim | `{ utr }` |
| confirm | `{ utr }` (the UTR the buyer claimed; server compares) |
| not received (seller) | `{ description? }` |
| refund (seller) | `{ refund_utr }` |
| open dispute | `{ reason, description?, utr?, screenshot? }` |
| fulfilment | `{ status: accepted\|packed\|shipped\|delivered\|rejected, reason? }` |
| cancel (buyer) | `{ reason? }` |
| resolve (admin) | `{ resolution, note }` (note required, ≤ 1000) |

UTR input is normalised by the server (spaces and `-` removed) and must then be exactly 12 digits; the client sends the normalised digits.

---

## 11. Claim · Dispute · Message · Evidence

### 11.1 Claim (`OrderView.claim`, nullable until the buyer submits a UTR)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `utr` | `utr` | `String` | no | 12 digits, globally unique. Display grouped `1234 5678 9012` |
| `amount_paise` | `amountPaise` | `int` | no | the order total at claim time |
| `claimed_at` | `claimedAt` | `DateTime` | no | |
| `updated_at` | `updatedAt` | `DateTime` | no | changes when the buyer edits the UTR |
| `refund_utr` | `refundUtr` | `String?` | yes | set when the seller records a refund |
| `refunded_at` | `refundedAt` | `DateTime?` | yes | |

(`refund_recent` is computed in SQL for the rules but is **not** in the view.)

### 11.2 Dispute (`OrderView.disputes[]`)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | `int` | no | |
| `origin_payment_status` | `originPaymentStatus` | `PaymentStatus` | no | the payment status the dispute was opened from; selects the resolution set (§15.5). Kept as a `String` fallback if unknown |
| `opened_by_role` | `openedByRole` | `String` | no | `buyer` \| `seller` |
| `reason` | `reason` | `DisputeReason` | no | §15 |
| `description` | `description` | `String?` | yes | ≤ 1000 chars |
| `status` | `status` | `DisputeStatus` | no | `open` \| `resolved` |
| `resolution` | `resolution` | `DisputeResolution?` | yes | null while open |
| `resolution_note` | `resolutionNote` | `String?` | yes | |
| `opened_at` | `openedAt` | `DateTime` | no | |
| `resolved_at` | `resolvedAt` | `DateTime?` | yes | |
| `proof_seconds_left` | `proofSecondsLeft` | `int?` | yes | raw `TIMESTAMPDIFF(SECOND, NOW(), opened_at + proof window)`, present on every dispute entry; meaningful only while `status == open` and `reason == not_received` (buyer's time to upload proof after a seller "not received"); can be ≤ 0 |

### 11.3 AdminDisputeRow (`GET /shop/admin/disputes?status=open|resolved` → `data.disputes[]`)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? |
|---|---|---|---|
| `id`, `order_id` | `id`, `orderId` | `int` | no |
| `reason`, `status`, `origin_payment_status`, `opened_by_role`, `description`, `resolution`, `resolution_note`, `opened_at`, `resolved_at` | as 11.2 | | per 11.2 |
| `order_number` | `orderNumber` | `String` | no |
| `total_paise` | `totalPaise` | `int` | no |
| `payment_status` | `paymentStatus` | `PaymentStatus` | no | current, may differ from origin |
| `order_status` | `orderStatus` | `OrderStatus` | no | (renamed from `status`) |
| `shop_name`, `buyer_username`, `seller_username` | | `String` | no |

Sorted open first, then newest. Max 500 rows. Resolve: `POST /shop/admin/disputes/:id/resolve` `{resolution, note}` → `{success, message: "Dispute resolved"}` (no `data`).

### 11.4 Message (`GET /shop/orders/:id/messages?after=<id>` → `data = { messages: [Message], read_only: bool }`; `POST` → `201`, `data.message`)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | `int` | no | monotonically increasing; poll with `?after=<lastId>` |
| `sender_role` | `senderRole` | `MessageSender` | no | `buyer`\|`seller`\|`admin`\|`system` |
| `sender_user_id` | `senderUserId` | `int?` | yes | null for `system` messages |
| `sender_username` | `senderUsername` | `String?` | yes | null for `system` |
| `body` | `body` | `String` | no | plain text, ≤ 1000; HTML tags are stripped by the server. **System messages are auto-generated English sentences** (e.g. "Seller marked the order as shipped.") — render as centred notices |
| `created_at` | `createdAt` | `DateTime` | no | |

Page size 200. Send body: `{ body: String }` (non-empty after trimming, ≤ 1000).

### 11.5 Evidence (`GET /shop/orders/:id/evidence` → `data.evidence[]`; `POST` → `201`, `data.evidence = { id, kind }`)

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Null? | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `id` | `id` | `int` | no | |
| `kind` | `kind` | `EvidenceKind` | no | `payment_proof`\|`refund_proof`\|`other` (buyer may post `payment_proof`/`other`, seller `refund_proof`/`other`; the other role's vocabulary value is coerced to `other`, a value outside the three kinds is a 400) |
| `created_at` | `createdAt` | `DateTime` | no | |
| `uploader_user_id` | `uploaderUserId` | `int` | no | |
| `uploader_username` | `uploaderUsername` | `String` | no | |
| `url` | `url` | `String` | no | **signed, expires in ~10 minutes** — fetch the list again to refresh; never persist |

`POST` body: `{ image: <data URL>, kind? }`.

---

## 12. ActionCounts and ShopConfig

**`GET /shop/orders/action-count`** → `data` (no wrapper key):

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `buyer` | `buyer` | `int` | unpaid UPI orders with time left |
| `seller` | `seller` | `int` | claimed / refund_due / fulfilment pending (0 if no shop) |
| `admin_disputes` | `adminDisputes` | `int` | open disputes (0 for non-admins) |
| `total` | `total` | `int` | sum — drives the tab badge |

**`GET /shop/config`** → `data`: `payment_methods: List<String>` (`["upi","cod"]`), `pay_window_minutes: int`, `confirm_window_hours: num` (in practice a whole number: `CONFIRM_WINDOW_HOURS` is parsed with `parseInt`, minimum 1), `max_open_upi_orders: int`. Use it for copy such as "Pay within 30 minutes" instead of hard-coding.

---

## 13. Paging

Present on `GET /profiles`, `GET /shop/shops`, `GET /shop/products` as `data.pagination`. Other lists (orders, addresses, categories, disputes, admin shops, messages) are **not paged** (fixed caps: buyer orders 200, seller orders 500, disputes 500, messages 200 per call).

| JSON key | Dart name | Dart type |
|---|---|---|
| `current_page` | `currentPage` | `int` |
| `per_page` | `perPage` | `int` |
| `total` | `total` | `int` |
| `total_pages` | `totalPages` | `int` |
| `has_next` | `hasNext` | `bool` |
| `has_prev` | `hasPrev` | `bool` |

Query parameters: `page` (default 1), `limit` (default 20 for shops/products; profiles default per backend, app uses 25; products max 100).
A page holder in the app (`Paged<T> { items, pagination }`) is a client class, not a server shape.

---

## 14. ApiError and envelope

```jsonc
// success
{ "success": true, "message": "Login successful", "data": { /* … */ } }
// failure
{ "success": false, "message": "Validation failed", "errors": ["Username must be between 3 and 50 characters", "…"] }
```

| Source | HTTP | `message` examples | `errors` |
|---|---|---|---|
| express-validator (`handleValidationErrors`) | 400 | `Validation failed` | list of plain strings (not keyed by field) |
| Business rule (`HttpError`) | 400/403/404/409/… | verbatim, user-facing English | absent |
| Auth middleware | 401 | `Access token required`, `Invalid token`, `Token expired`, `Invalid token - user not found` | absent |
| Admin gate | 403 | `Admin access required` | absent |
| Shop gates | 403 | `You do not have a shop yet. Apply to become a shop owner first.`, `Your shop is <status>. Only approved shops can manage products and orders.` | absent |
| Login limiter / chat limiter | 429 | `Too many failed login attempts. Please try again in 15 minutes.` / `You are sending messages too quickly. Please wait a minute.` | absent |
| Body too large | 413 | `Request body is too large` | absent |
| Malformed JSON | 400 | `Malformed JSON in request body` | absent |
| Unexpected | 500 | `Something went wrong. Please try again.` / `Internal server error` / controller-specific (`Failed to …`) | absent (`error`/`stack` only in development: never display) |

```dart
class ApiException implements Exception {
  final int? status;          // null for offline/timeout
  final String message;       // server message verbatim, or app copy for offline/timeout
  final List<String> errors;  // validation messages, may be empty
  final ApiErrorKind kind;    // validation|unauthorized|forbidden|notFound|conflict|rateLimited|tooLarge|server|offline|timeout|unknown
}
```

A 401 on any non-login call means the session is over (doc 01 §4). Messages are the contract the UI shows; do not rewrite them.

---

## 15. Enums (exact strings)

Every enum gets a `fromJson(String?)` that maps unknown values to `unknown` (never throws) and a `value` getter returning the wire string. The app must **not** invent states: render unknown states as a neutral chip with the raw text.

### 15.1 Roles and viewer roles
| Enum | Values |
|---|---|
| `UserRole` | `admin`, `user`, `profile_holder` |
| `ViewerRole` (order view) | `buyer`, `seller`, `admin` |
| `MessageSender` | `buyer`, `seller`, `admin`, `system` |
| `CancelledBy` | `buyer`, `seller`, `system`, `admin` |

### 15.2 Profile
`ProfileLevel`: `parish`, `deanery`, `dexco` (stored free-form `VARCHAR(50)` — treat unknown as `parish` background but keep the raw value). Designations: see `assets/data/designations.json` (13 values).

### 15.3 Shop and product
| Enum | Values | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| `ShopStatus` | `pending`, `approved`, `rejected`, `suspended` | UI adds client state `none` when `shop == null` |
| `ProductStatus` | `draft`, `active`, `out_of_stock`, `archived` | |
| `PaymentMethod` | `upi`, `cod` | |

### 15.4 Order and payment
| Enum | Values |
|---|---|
| `OrderStatus` | `pending_payment`, `placed`, `accepted`, `packed`, `shipped`, `delivered`, `cancelled`, `rejected` |
| `PaymentStatus` | `awaiting_payment`, `claimed`, `confirmed`, `disputed`, `refund_due`, `refunded`, `void`, `written_off`, `cod_pending`, `cod_collected` |

Notes: a new UPI order starts `pending_payment` + `awaiting_payment`; a new COD order starts `placed` + `cod_pending`. When the seller confirms a UPI payment the status moves to `placed`. Legacy value `paid`/`pending`/`failed` no longer exist after the migration. Fulfilment steps allowed by the server: `placed → accepted → packed → shipped → delivered` (and `rejected`/`cancelled` as terminals). Terminal payment states: `void`, `refunded`, `written_off`, `cod_collected` (and `confirmed` once `delivered`). The full state machine, timers and transitions are in doc 06.

### 15.5 Disputes
| Enum | Values |
|---|---|
| `DisputeReason` | `not_received`, `not_confirmed`, `wrong_amount`, `refund_not_received`, `paid_after_cancel`, `not_delivered`, `other` |
| `DisputeStatus` | `open`, `resolved` |
| `DisputeResolution` | `payment_confirmed`, `claim_rejected`, `refund_required`, `fulfilment_ordered`, `refund_confirmed`, `closed_unrecoverable` (the backend also records `claim_rejected` automatically when the buyer misses the proof deadline) |
| `EvidenceKind` | `payment_proof`, `refund_proof`, `other` |

Resolutions an admin may choose, by `origin_payment_status` (from `RESOLUTIONS_BY_ORIGIN`; the UI offers exactly this set for the dispute's origin — a server 400 means the list drifted):

| Origin | Allowed resolutions |
|---|---|
| `claimed` | `payment_confirmed`, `claim_rejected`, `refund_required` |
| `void` | `refund_required`, `claim_rejected` |
| `confirmed` | `refund_required`, `fulfilment_ordered` |
| `refund_due` | `refund_confirmed`, `refund_required`, `closed_unrecoverable` |
| `refunded` | `refund_confirmed`, `refund_required`, `closed_unrecoverable` |

### 15.6 `allowed_actions` vocabulary (strings in `OrderView.allowed_actions`)

| Action | Who | Meaning / which endpoint (doc 05) |
|---|---|---|
| `claim` | buyer | submit UTR → `POST /shop/orders/:id/payment-claim` |
| `edit_claim` | buyer | correct UTR → `PATCH …/payment-claim` |
| `cancel` | buyer | `PUT …/cancel` |
| `confirm` | seller | `POST …/payment-confirm` |
| `reject` | seller | `PUT …/status` with `status: rejected` + reason |
| `advance:accepted`, `advance:packed`, `advance:shipped`, `advance:delivered` | seller | `PUT …/status` with the suffix as `status` |
| `refund` | seller | `POST …/refund` |
| `resolve` | admin | `POST /shop/admin/disputes/:id/resolve` |
| `chat` | buyer/seller/admin(with dispute) | messages enabled |
| `upload_evidence` | buyer/seller | `POST …/evidence` |
| `dispute` | buyer/seller | at least one entry in `dispute_reasons` |

Parse `advance:<x>` by splitting on `:`; ignore any action string the app does not know. Seller "not received" (`POST …/payment-not-received`) and "wrong amount" are **dispute reasons** (`dispute_reasons` of a seller contains `not_received`, `wrong_amount`, `other`), not entries of `allowed_actions`; buyer dispute reasons: `not_confirmed`, `paid_after_cancel`, `refund_not_received`, `not_delivered`, `other`.

---

## 16. Illustrative snippet — `Profile` (uncompiled)

```dart
class Profile {
  const Profile({
    required this.id, required this.name, this.fatherName, this.motherName, this.dateOfBirth,
    this.designation, this.level, this.dateOfBaptism, this.postalAddress, this.parish, this.deanery,
    this.qualification, this.phone, this.involvement, this.photoUrl, this.issueDate,
    required this.status, this.profileUserId, this.createdBy, this.createdByUsername,
  });

  final int id;
  final String name;            // lowercase from server; format for display
  final String? fatherName;     // JSON READ key: 'father'
  final String? motherName;     // JSON READ key: 'mother'
  final String? dateOfBirth;    // JSON READ key: 'dob' (calendar date, YYYY-MM-DD)
  final String? designation, level, dateOfBaptism, postalAddress, parish, deanery;
  final String? qualification, phone, involvement, photoUrl, issueDate;
  final int status;             // 1 active, 0 deleted
  final int? profileUserId, createdBy;
  final String? createdByUsername; // only on list/detail

  factory Profile.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> j) => Profile(
        id: asInt(j['id']),
        name: j['name'] as String,
        fatherName: j['father'] as String?,
        motherName: j['mother'] as String?,
        dateOfBirth: asCalendarDate(j['dob']),
        designation: j['designation'] as String?,
        level: j['level'] as String?,
        dateOfBaptism: asCalendarDate(j['date_of_baptism']),
        postalAddress: j['postal_address'] as String?,
        parish: j['parish'] as String?,
        deanery: j['deanery'] as String?,
        qualification: j['qualification'] as String?,
        phone: j['phone'] as String?,
        involvement: j['involvement'] as String?,
        photoUrl: j['photo_url'] as String?,
        issueDate: asCalendarDate(j['issue_date']),
        status: asInt(j['status']),
        profileUserId: asIntOrNull(j['profile_user_id']),
        createdBy: asIntOrNull(j['created_by']),
        createdByUsername: j['created_by_username'] as String?,
      );
}

/// The form → request mapping: WRITE names differ from READ names. Keep it next to the model.
Map<String, dynamic> profileToRequest(ProfileFormData f) => {
      'name': f.name.trim(),
      'father_name': f.fatherName,        // not 'father'
      'mother_name': f.motherName,        // not 'mother'
      'date_of_birth': f.dateOfBirth,     // not 'dob'; required in practice
      'designation': f.designation,
      'level': f.level,
      'date_of_baptism': f.dateOfBaptism,
      'postal_address': f.postalAddress,
      'parish': f.parish,
      'deanery': f.deanery,
      'qualification': f.qualification,
      'phone': f.phone,
      'involvement': f.involvement,
      if (f.photoDataUrl != null) 'photo': f.photoDataUrl, // mandatory on create
    };
```

Required unit tests for this doc (doc 10 §2): a round-trip test that `profileToRequest` never emits `father`/`mother`/`dob`; `Profile.fromJson` never reads `father_name`/`mother_name`/`date_of_birth`; `asBool` for `0/1/true/false`; `OrderView.fromJson` with `payee: null`, `claim: null`, unknown `payment_status`, and an unknown `allowed_actions` string.

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/09-IMPLEMENTATION-PLAN.md ===== -->


# 09 · Implementation plan — milestones M0 → M10

How the app is built, in order. The **main Claude Code session is the orchestrator**: it reads this
plan, delegates each milestone's work to the named specialist agents (`.claude/agents/`), runs the
reviewers, and ticks the tracker at the bottom. (Subagents cannot launch subagents — the orchestrator
does all delegation. See `prompts/00-MASTER-BUILD-PROMPT.md`.)

Effort figures are rough *working days for one developer driving Claude Code*, to help sequencing — not commitments.

## Global Definition of Done (applies to every milestone)

A task is done only when **all** hold:
1. `flutter analyze` has no issues; `dart format --set-exit-if-changed .` passes.
2. New logic has unit tests; new screens have widget tests for loading/empty/error/data states; screens specified with exact visuals have golden tests (dark + light, 390 px; 360 px for dense screens).
3. Every endpoint used is in `docs/05-API-CONTRACT.md` with the exact path/method/body; the **api-contract-verifier** reports no mismatches for the touched repositories.
4. Every screen matches its spec section (copy, states, measurements); the **ui-fidelity-reviewer** reports no blocking findings.
5. The **flutter-code-reviewer** reports no blocking findings (architecture rules in doc 01 §12).
6. Verified at least once against the **local backend** with seeded data (`tools/`), for each role the screen concerns.
7. The tracker below and `docs/11-DESIGN-VS-BACKEND.md` (if a new conflict appeared) are updated.
8. No secrets, tokens, base64 or personal data in logs, tests, fixtures or commits.

## Dependency graph

```
M0 ─► M1 ─► M2 ─┬─► M3 ─► M4 ─► M5
                ├─► M6 ─► M7 ─► M8
                └──────────────────► M9 ─► M10
```
M3–M5 (ID cards) and M6–M8 (shop) are independent after M2 and may run in parallel if two sessions/people are available.

---

## M0 — Scaffold and foundations  *(owner: app-architect · ~1 day)*
**Goal:** an empty-but-correct app that builds, runs on an emulator, and has every convention in place.
**Tasks**
1. `flutter create` (org chosen by the owner, project name `cyd_app`, android + ios), copy the kit in (see README), **delete the generated `test/widget_test.dart`** (it will not compile once `main.dart` changes), commit.
2. Add packages — M0 set only: `flutter pub add flutter_riverpod go_router dio flutter_secure_storage shared_preferences google_fonts intl url_launcher cached_network_image image_picker image` and `flutter pub add --dev flutter_lints mocktail http_mock_adapter` plus `flutter pub add --dev "integration_test:{sdk: flutter}"`. Defer `pdf`, `printing`, `share_plus`, the gallery-save plugin to M4. Commit `pubspec.lock`.
3. Create the folder layout of doc 01 §2 with placeholder files; set up `analysis_options.yaml` (`flutter_lints` + rules from doc 01 §12).
4. Register assets in `pubspec.yaml` (under `flutter:` → `assets:` list `assets/idcard/`, `assets/data/`; under `fonts:` declare family `Gafata` → `assets/fonts/Gafata-Regular.ttf`) — Vidaloka/Roboto are added in M4; verify the three card images load.
5. `core/config/env.dart` with `API_BASE_URL`/`FLAVOR` defines; `core/network` skeleton (dio provider, error mapper, envelope parser) with unit tests using a mock adapter.
6. `core/storage` wrappers; `core/format` (money, dates, text) **with unit tests** (cases in doc 06 §Formats).
7. `main.dart` + `MaterialApp.router` + temporary router with `/splash`, `/login` placeholder.
8. CI workflow (GitHub Actions): `flutter pub get`, analyze, format check, test.
9. Debug-only cleartext access for the local backend: Android → `android/app/src/debug/AndroidManifest.xml` with `android:usesCleartextTraffic="true"` on `<application>` (the emulator reaches the host at `http://10.0.2.2:3000`); iOS → `NSAllowsLocalNetworking` under `NSAppTransportSecurity` in `Info.plist` (lets the simulator reach `localhost`). Release builds stay HTTPS-only (doc 13).
10. Append to `.gitignore`: `.env`, `key.properties`, `*.jks`, `*.keystore`, `.claude/settings.local.json`. Add `/debug/health` (calls `GET /health`) as a debug-only route to prove connectivity from the emulator, plus the "DEV" ribbon for `FLAVOR=dev`.
**Acceptance:** `flutter run` shows a placeholder; `flutter test` green; CI green; `GET /health` reachable from the emulator; assets load; no secrets committed.
**Risks:** package version conflicts → resolve with `pub add`, record decisions in `docs/DECISIONS.md` (create it).

## M1 — Design system and shell  *(owner: design-system-engineer · ~3 days)*
**Goal:** the visual foundation every screen uses.
**Tasks**
1. `CydTokens` ThemeExtension from `design/tokens.json` (dark + light); `ThemeData` builders; theme mode controller (System/Light/Dark) persisted.
2. Fonts via `google_fonts` (Anton/Archivo/DM Mono) + the type ladder from doc 02; offline fallbacks.
3. Core widgets (doc 02 catalogue): buttons, chips, status chips (order **and** payment **and** card-validity), cards, inputs, radio/switch/stepper, price text, skeleton, empty/error cards, `AsyncListView`, bottom sheet, dialog, toast, copy-row, countdown pill, section label, avatar, app bars.
4. `ShellScaffold` + **floating pill tab bar** (5 tabs, badge, per-role first tab label/icon, hide rules).
5. A hidden "Design gallery" debug route showing every widget in both themes.
6. Golden tests for each widget (dark/light).
**Acceptance:** gallery matches doc 02 measurements; goldens committed; tab bar behaves per doc 02 (blur, selected pill, badge, hide on sub-screens).
**Depends on:** M0.

## M2 — Session, auth and network  *(owner: auth-network-engineer · ~3 days)*
**Goal:** log in as any role and land on the right first tab with a working, secure HTTP layer.
**Tasks**
1. `token_store`, `session_controller` (restore, login, logout, expire), redirect guard in the router.
2. `AuthRepository`: `POST /auth/login` for all roles; map errors (`Invalid credentials`, 429 rate limit copy).
3. Login screen per doc 04 (show/hide password, loading text "Logging in…", error banner, rate-limit message).
4. Splash/session restore; expiry handling (401 → clear → login with toast `Session expired. Please login again.`).
5. Role-based first-tab root (`IdHomeByRole`), Account tab stub with sign-out (clears token, user, cart, caches).
6. Cold-start "Waking up the server…" hint; offline detection banner.
**Acceptance:** each seeded account logs in and sees the right first tab (the holder lands on **My ID**; admin, manager, buyer and sellers all have role `admin`/`user` and land on **Profiles**); wrong password shows the exact message; 11 wrong attempts show the 15-minute message; killing the token (edit secure storage or wait) returns to login; sign-out leaves no personal data behind.
**Depends on:** M1.

## M3 — Profiles: list, filters, stats, form  *(owner: idcard-engineer · ~6 days)*
**Goal:** admin/user can find, create, edit, delete profiles.
**Tasks**
1. `ProfilesRepository`: `GET /profiles` (page, limit, search, deanery, parish, level, designation, sort), `GET /profiles/filter-options`, `GET /profiles/stats`, `GET /profiles/:id`, `POST /profiles`, `PUT /profiles/:id`, `DELETE /profiles/:id`.
2. Profiles screen: stats tiles (admin) / fallback (user), debounced search (500 ms), filter sheet (deanery→parish dependency), active-filter chips, infinite-scroll list (limit 25) with skeleton/empty/error, pull-to-refresh, cached first page offline.
3. Profile form (create/edit): all 14 fields, verbatim validation, dependent parish list from `assets/data/deaneries.json`, date pickers (`YYYY-MM-DD`), mandatory photo with client resize, exact write/read field mapping (`father_name`↔`father` …).
4. Credentials dialog after create (copy/share; restore-by-recreation notice).
5. Delete with confirmation; profile detail screen.
**Acceptance:** doc 04 checklists for Profiles/Form pass for `admin` and `user` (a `user` only sees profiles they created); creating a profile returns credentials and the holder can log in; deleting disables the holder login; validation messages match verbatim; list handles 1000 rows smoothly.
**Depends on:** M2. **Note:** photo upload needs Cloudinary configured on the local backend.

## M4 — ID card rendering, export and My ID  *(owner: idcard-engineer · ~6 days)*
**Goal:** the card looks exactly like the printed design and can be shared/printed; holders see their own.
**Tasks**
0. Download and register the card fonts (Vidaloka, Roboto; Gafata is in the kit) and assert they are loaded before any export.
1. Card widget per doc 07 (3 level backgrounds, mm→px scale, fonts, value rows, photo, footer) + long-text policy.
2. Golden tests with the six fixtures F1–F6 of doc 07 §14.3 (each level; long name/parish/address; edge data; photo crop).
3. Preview screen (zoom/pan), Share PNG, Save to gallery, PDF at 146.3×221.8 mm + print; filename rule.
4. **My ID** screen for `profile_holder`: `GET /profile-holder/my-profile` + `/my-id-card`, validity chip (Valid / Expires in n days / Expired, 60-day rule, 2 years), renewal copy, card preview, update photo (`PUT /profile-holder/update-profile`), offline cached card.
5. Admin/user: open any profile's card from the list.
**Acceptance:** side-by-side with the web card for the same data matches within 1 mm; exported PNG is 1728×2620 (the "original quality" option may be 2744×4160); PDF page is exactly the card size; holder can see and share their card offline after one online load.
**Depends on:** M3 (profile data) and M1.

## M5 — Account area  *(owners: idcard-engineer + auth-network-engineer · ~3 days)*
**Tasks:** account info view/edit (`GET/PUT /auth/profile`), change password (`PUT /auth/change-password`, `PUT /profile-holder/change-password` for holders; strict rules in the UI, strength meter), create user (admin, `POST /auth/register`), appearance (System/Light/Dark), sign-out dialog, role-specific entries (shop entries are added in M6/M8).
**Acceptance:** doc 04 Account checklist for all roles; password rules verbatim; holder cannot see admin-only rows.
**Depends on:** M2, M3 (for holder pieces).

## M6 — Shop browse, cart, addresses  *(owner: shop-customer-engineer · ~6 days)*
**Tasks**
1. Repositories: categories, shops (list/detail), products (list/detail/lookup), addresses CRUD.
2. Shop home (hero, search entry, category rail, promo cards (static), featured shops, product grid), Categories, Search & Filter (query, category, shops section with deanery, client sort/price), Storefront, Product detail (gallery, stock chip, stepper, add/buy now), wishlist (local).
3. Cart: per-user persisted controller, lookup revalidation with change notices, grouped by shop, stepper, undo, multi-shop banner.
4. Addresses list/form (landmark → `line2`), default handling, delete confirm.
**Acceptance:** doc 03 sections for these screens match (loading/empty/error everywhere); cart survives restart, is separate per user, clears on sign-out; price change on the server is detected at checkout; address CRUD works against the backend.
**Depends on:** M2.

## M7 — Checkout, peer-to-peer payment, orders (buyer)  *(owner: shop-customer-engineer · ~8 days)*
**Tasks**
1. Checkout (steps Address → Payment (per shop, `accepts_upi`) → Review), `POST /shop/checkout` with stable Idempotency-Key, routing to Pay / Pay-your-orders / Confirmation.
2. Pay the seller screen (copy UPI ID/amount/reference, countdown, `upi://` intent, QR, UTR form, screenshot, claim/edit claim), waiting/expired/confirmed/disputed states, polling.
3. Orders list (filters, needs-action), Order detail (role-aware; buyer part): banners by state, cancel, dispute sheet, refund-not-received.
4. Order chat (poll `?after=` 5 s) and evidence upload/view (signed URLs).
5. Action badge on the **Shop** tab (doc 02 §9.19; Cart shows the quantity badge) from `action-count`.
**Acceptance:** the full timeline in doc 06 §Worked example passes end-to-end on the local backend with the shortened timers; the buyer can never perform an action the server did not offer; double-tapping Place order creates one set of orders; expiry returns the buyer to a "no longer waiting" state.
**Depends on:** M6.

## M8 — Seller and admin  *(owner: shop-seller-admin-engineer · ~8 days)*
**Tasks**
1. Become a seller form + the five states (none/pending/rejected/suspended/approved) from `GET /shop/shops/mine`.
2. My shop: stats, Products tab (list, status chips, edit, archive), Product form (up to 6 images, categories, price/MRP/stock/status, full-body PUT), Orders tab, Shop profile tab (logo/banner, fields, password-gated UPI change).
3. Seller order handling in Order detail: confirm payment (sends the claim UTR), not received → dispute, reject (reason), refund form, fulfilment steps from `allowed_actions`; suspended-shop orders-only mode.
4. Admin: Shop applications (filters, review sheet, approve/reject/suspend/reinstate with reasons), Shop disputes (queue, detail with chat + evidence, resolve with note per origin).
**Acceptance:** doc 03 seller/admin checklists pass; admin resolves each dispute type end-to-end; seller of a suspended shop can still ship/refund; buyer–seller–admin visibility rules hold (admin sees chat only with a dispute).
**Depends on:** M7 (shared order screen).

## M9 — Quality pass  *(owners: test-engineer, api-contract-verifier, ui-fidelity-reviewer, flutter-code-reviewer · ~6 days)*
**Tasks:** full contract verification against the live local backend; UI fidelity sweep (every screen, dark/light, 360/390/412 px, text scale 1.3); accessibility (labels, contrast, tap targets, screen reader order); performance (list jank, memory with card export, image sizes); offline and poor-network behaviour; integration tests per role; security review (logs, storage, cleartext, secrets); fix everything blocking; update docs.
**Acceptance:** doc 10 checklists all pass; zero blocking findings from the three reviewers; integration suite green.
**Depends on:** M5 and M8.

## M10 — Release  *(owner: release-engineer · ~4 days)*
**Tasks:** identifiers and icons (owner input needed), flavors, Android signing + Play App Signing, iOS signing, versioning, privacy policy and data-safety answers, store listing assets, CI build artifacts, internal testing track, staged rollout plan, support contact. Details in doc 13.
**Acceptance:** a signed release build installs on a clean device, points to the production HTTPS URL, passes the smoke checklist, and the store checklists in doc 13 are complete.

---

## Cross-cutting tasks (assign when relevant)
- **Strings**: collect user-visible text in `core/strings.dart` from the first screen on.
- **Accessibility**: semantics labels for every icon-only button and glyph (doc 02).
- **Analytics/crash**: none until M10 decision.
- **Decision log**: `docs/DECISIONS.md` — one line per non-obvious decision (package choice, deviation, new conflict).

## Risk register
| Risk | Impact | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Local backend lacks Cloudinary → photo flows can't be tested | M3/M4/M8 blocked | Owner provides a Cloudinary dev account; until then test with API-created profiles (no photo) and mock uploads |
| Render cold starts make demos feel broken | UX | Wake-up hint, retry GET once, keep the Render instance warm during demos |
| Card text overflow for long parish/address | Ugly cards | Long-text policy in doc 07 + golden fixtures |
| Rules drift between app and backend (order actions) | Bugs | Render only from `allowed_actions`; contract verifier each milestone |
| iOS/Android differences (UPI intents, gallery save, permissions) | Late surprises | Test on both from M4 on; doc 13 permission list |
| Backend PRs not merged yet | App built on a branch | Develop on `claude/happy-bell-oy6or2`; re-verify after merge (M9) |
| Owner sign-off on invented screens late | Rework | Share screenshots after M1 (tokens), M3/M4 (ID area), M7 (payment) |

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/10-TESTING-QA.md ===== -->


# 10 · Testing and QA

## 1. Strategy at a glance

| Layer | Tooling | What it proves | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unit | `flutter_test`, `mocktail` | formatters, validators, cart maths, UTR normalisation, state-machine view logic, model `fromJson` | `test/core`, `test/models`, `test/features/*/application` |
| Repository | `dio` + `http_mock_adapter` (or a custom `HttpClientAdapter`) with **recorded-shape fixtures from doc 05** | request path/method/body/headers and response parsing, error mapping | `test/features/*/data` |
| Widget | `flutter_test` | each screen's loading / empty / error / data states, validation messages, button enablement, role gating | `test/features/*/presentation` |
| Golden | `flutter_test` goldens (fixed fonts, 390×844 and 360×800, dark + light) | pixel fidelity of design-system widgets, the ID card (5 fixtures) and key screens | `test/golden/` |
| Integration | `integration_test` on an emulator against the **local backend** seeded by `tools/seed-dev-data.js` | real flows per role (below) | `integration_test/` |
| Contract | the **api-contract-verifier** agent (read-only) + a small script that diffs `docs/05` paths vs repositories | no endpoint/field drift | run at every milestone |
| Manual QA | checklists in §5 on a real Android phone (and iOS before release) | what automation cannot see | owner / tester |

Rules: tests never hit production; fixtures contain no real personal data; screenshots/goldens never contain real photos; a
bug fix starts with a failing test.

## 2. Unit and repository test must-haves

- **Money**: `formatPaise(0)`, `5`, `100`, `12345`, `100000` (₹1,000.00), negatives rejected; no floats anywhere.
- **Dates**: `DD/MM/YYYY` (lists), `DD-MM-YYYY` (card), `MMM DD, YYYY` (joined); issue date + 2 years; validity edges (61 days left → valid, 60 → "Expires in 60 days" warning, the expiry day itself and the day after exactly as defined in doc 07, a null `issue_date` handled without crashing).
- **capitalizeName**: `JOHN DOE`, `o'neil`, `mary-ann`, multiple spaces, empty/null-safe.
- **UTR**: `4012 3456 7890` → `401234567890`; rejects 11/13 digits, letters; grouping `4012 3456 7890`.
- **Cart**: per-user keys; add/merge lines; clamp to stock and 99; `applyLookup` removes unavailable lines, updates price/stock/shop name, reports changes; sign-out clears only that user's cart.
- **Idempotency key**: stable across retries, rotated on cart or method change.
- **Validators**: every message verbatim from doc 06 (profile 14 fields, phone exactly 10 digits, password rules, address, shop apply, product).
- **Order view logic**: for a table of `OrderView` fixtures (each status × payment_status × viewer role), the visible buttons equal `allowed_actions` and nothing else.
- **Error mapping**: 400 with `errors[]`, 401 (login vs other), 403, 404, 409, 413, 429, 5xx, timeout, offline.
- **Repositories**: every endpoint in doc 05 has a request-shape test (path, method, query, body keys, headers incl. `Idempotency-Key`) and a response-parse test using the example JSON from doc 05.

## 3. Golden tests

1. Design-system gallery widgets (every state, dark/light).
2. **ID card** — six fixtures F1–F6 from doc 07 §14.3 (parish, deanery, dexco; very long name; very long parish + address; edge data; photo crop). Use the bundled fonts so goldens are stable across machines.
3. Key screens: Login, Profiles (data/empty), Profile form (error state), My ID, Shop home, Product, Cart, Checkout step 3, **Pay the seller**, Order detail (one fixture per payment state), Seller confirm sheet, Admin dispute resolve, Account (per role).
Update goldens only with an explicit reviewer-approved reason in the PR/commit message.

## 4. Integration tests (against the local backend)

Prereqs: local backend + seed (`tools/LOCAL-BACKEND.md`) with short timers
(`PAY_WINDOW_MINUTES=3`, `PAY_GRACE_MINUTES=1`, `CONFIRM_WINDOW_HOURS=1`). Each test logs in through the UI, never by injecting tokens.

| Test | Role(s) | Flow |
|---|---|---|
| `auth_flow` | all | login success/failure, session restore after app restart, sign-out clears data, forced expiry |
| `profiles_crud` | manager | create → credentials → find with search/filter → edit → delete → holder login blocked |
| `holder_card` | holder | login → My ID → card renders → share sheet opens → offline (airplane) still shows cached card |
| `shop_browse_cart` | buyer | browse → search → product → add → cart survives restart → price change on server → warning at checkout |
| `checkout_cod` | buyer + seller | COD order → seller advances to delivered → `cod_collected` |
| `p2p_happy_path` | buyer + seller | UPI checkout → copy UPI ID → submit UTR → seller confirms → fulfilment → delivered |
| `p2p_expiry` | buyer | place UPI order → wait window+grace → order cancelled, stock back, buyer sees "no longer waiting" |
| `p2p_dispute` | buyer, seller, admin | claim → seller "not received" → buyer proof → admin resolves (each resolution) |
| `p2p_refund` | buyer, seller | confirmed order → seller rejects → refund UTR → buyer sees refunded |
| `seller_onboarding` | seller + admin | apply → admin approves → add product (with images if Cloudinary configured) → appears in marketplace |
| `suspension` | seller + admin | suspend shop → unpaid order voided, paid order still fulfillable, shop hidden |
| `multi_shop_checkout` | buyer | cart across two shops → one checkout → two orders → pay both |
| `double_submit` | buyer | double-tap Place order → exactly one set of orders |

Run: `flutter test integration_test --dart-define=API_BASE_URL=http://10.0.2.2:3000` (Android emulator).

## 5. Manual QA checklists (per role) — use the seeded accounts

Accounts (from `tools/seed-dev-data.js`): `admin/admin123`, `cydmanager/Passw0rd1`, `seller1/Passw0rd1`,
`seller2/Passw0rd1` (COD-only), `buyer1/Passw0rd1`, holder `anil1304/9000000001`.
Mark each item ✔/✘ and record the device, OS, app version and backend commit.

### 5.1 Everyone
- [ ] First launch: splash → login; no flash of protected content.
- [ ] Wrong password shows `Invalid credentials`; 11 wrong attempts show the 15-minute message.
- [ ] Airplane mode on each tab shows the offline banner/error card with retry; recovers when back online.
- [ ] Dark and light themes both readable; System follows the OS; text scale 1.3 does not clip critical text.
- [ ] Rotate to landscape (if supported) does not crash; small phone (360 px) has no overflow.
- [ ] Sign out → relaunch → lands on login; cart and personal caches gone.
- [ ] Session expired (token removed/expired) returns to login with the toast.
- [ ] Back button behaviour is natural everywhere; no dead ends.
- [ ] Screen reader (TalkBack) can reach and operate primary actions; every icon button has a label.

### 5.2 Profile holder (`anil1304`)
- [ ] Lands on **My ID**; profile summary correct; card renders with the right level colour and values.
- [ ] Validity chip: Valid / Expires in N days / Expired (test by editing `issue_date` in the DB).
- [ ] Share PNG, save to gallery, print/PDF work; file name `{name}-{parish}-{phone}`.
- [ ] Update photo (needs Cloudinary): picker, resize, upload, card updates.
- [ ] Other fields are read-only with "Contact CYD Office to change".
- [ ] Change password: strength meter, rules verbatim, old password then fails, new works.
- [ ] Holder cannot see Profiles/Create user/admin rows.
- [ ] Shop tabs work as a buyer.

### 5.3 ID-card manager (`cydmanager`, role `user`)
- [ ] Lands on **Profiles**; sees only profiles they created.
- [ ] Search (debounced), each filter, parish disabled until deanery chosen, clear filters, pagination/infinite scroll.
- [ ] Create profile: all validation messages; parish list follows deanery; photo mandatory; success dialog shows credentials with copy buttons; the new holder can log in.
- [ ] Re-create a deleted profile with the same name + phone → restore notice behaviour; old login works with new phone as password.
- [ ] Edit prefill correct (father/mother/dob mapping); unchanged photo keeps working.
- [ ] Delete: confirm copy; holder can no longer log in.
- [ ] Stats area degrades gracefully for non-admin.
- [ ] Card preview/export for any profile.

### 5.4 Admin (`admin`)
- [ ] Sees all profiles; stats tiles show total / with photos / today / this week (note: includes deleted).
- [ ] Create user (`user`/`admin`); the new account logs in; `profile_holder` not offered.
- [ ] Shop applications: filters, review sheet, approve, reject (reason required), suspend (reason), reinstate.
- [ ] Shop disputes: queue open/resolved, detail shows order, UTR, screenshots (signed links), chat; each resolution requires a note and only offers valid options for the dispute's origin; action badge counts open disputes.
- [ ] Cannot read an order's chat when there is no dispute.

### 5.5 Buyer (`buyer1`)
- [ ] Shop home: skeleton → content; categories; search with filters; storefront; product (gallery, stock chip, quantity limits).
- [ ] Cart: add from two shops → multi-shop banner; quantity stepper clamps; remove + Undo; total correct; survives restart; separate from another user's cart.
- [ ] Change a price in the DB → open cart → change notice → totals updated.
- [ ] Checkout: address add/select, per-shop method, COD-only shop disables UPI, review totals, Place order once only.
- [ ] **Pay the seller**: UPI ID/amount/reference copy work; countdown ticks and matches the server; Open UPI app launches a UPI app (device with one) or fails gracefully; QR scans; UTR validation (12 digits, spaces ignored); duplicate UTR message; screenshot attach; submit → waiting state.
- [ ] Edit UTR while waiting; after the seller confirms the field is no longer editable.
- [ ] Let an order expire → "no longer waiting" + "Already paid? Report it" path (paid-after-cancel dispute).
- [ ] Chat with seller: send/receive (5 s poll), system messages styled, read-only after closure.
- [ ] Orders: filters, needs-action indicator, status chips (order + payment), cancel only when offered.
- [ ] Open a dispute after the confirm window (shorten timer) with screenshot.
- [ ] Addresses: add/edit/set default/delete; past orders keep their copy.
- [ ] Become a seller from Account.

### 5.6 Seller (`seller1` with UPI, `seller2` COD-only)
- [ ] Apply (blank UPI allowed → COD-only); states: pending, rejected (reason), suspended, approved; they appear from `GET /shop/shops/mine`.
- [ ] My shop: stats, products list with status chips; add product (up to 6 images, MRP < price rejected client-side, full-body update works); archive confirm.
- [ ] Orders tab: "needs action" first; open a claimed order: confirm requires seeing amount + UTR; confirming with a changed UTR is refused.
- [ ] "Not received" opens a dispute; reject a paid order → refund form requires a refund UTR different from the payment UTR.
- [ ] Fulfilment steps appear one at a time; after delivered nothing left to do.
- [ ] Change UPI ID asks for the password; wrong password refused; open orders keep the old payee.
- [ ] Suspended shop: banner + orders-only dashboard; can still ship/refund.

### 5.7 Payment-timer scripts (with the short-timer backend)
1. **Expiry**: place UPI order, do nothing for window + grace → order cancelled, stock returned (check product detail).
2. **Seller silence**: claim payment, wait `CONFIRM_WINDOW_HOURS` → buyer sees "not confirmed in time" and can dispute.
3. **No proof**: seller "not received", buyer uploads nothing for `DISPUTE_PROOF_HOURS` → claim rejected automatically.

## 6. Non-functional checks
- **Performance**: cold start < 3 s on a mid-range phone (excluding network); list scroll 60 fps with 1000 rows; ID card export < 2 s and no OOM at the largest export size in doc 07 (2744×4160); images are downscaled before decode.
- **Size**: release APK/AAB size noted; card backgrounds are the biggest assets (≈ 10.8 MB) — consider shipping a reduced-resolution copy for on-screen display and the full one for export.
- **Security**: no secrets in the repo; `adb logcat`/Xcode console shows no tokens, UTRs or base64; release build is HTTPS-only; backups exclude secure storage where possible.
- **Privacy**: no third-party SDKs; camera/photo permissions requested only when used, with clear rationale text.
- **Compatibility**: Android 8+ (API 26+) phones from at least two vendors; iOS 15+ (final matrix agreed with the owner).

## 7. Bug report template
```
Title:
Build/commit:            Backend commit/branch:
Device + OS:             Role/account:
Steps (numbered):
Expected:                Actual:
Evidence (screenshot with personal data blurred):
Spec reference (doc + section / DB-xx row):
Severity: blocker / major / minor / cosmetic
```

## 8. Release acceptance sign-off
All of: Definition of Done met for M0–M9; §4 integration suite green; §5 checklists complete with no open blocker/major; contract verifier clean against the **merged** backend; owner visual sign-off on the invented screens (ID-card area, P2P payment, chat, disputes); privacy/data-safety answers reviewed; release build smoke-tested on a clean device.

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/11-DESIGN-VS-BACKEND.md ===== -->


# 11 · Mockup ↔ backend conflicts and how the app resolves them

The mockup (`design/CYD Shop Mobile.dc.html`, plus its `ORIGINAL-FLUTTER-STRUCTURE-PROPOSAL.md`) was
built from the **earlier** shop documentation (gateway payments, a server cart). The backend was later
rebuilt. **When the mockup and the backend disagree, the backend wins and the mockup's *look* is kept.**
This file lists every disagreement found, the decision, and where it is specified. Rows are numbered so
agents and reviewers can cite them (e.g. "per DB-07").

Legend — **Decision**: *Backend* = follow the backend; *Mockup* = keep the mockup; *New* = designed here;
*Client* = implemented client-side because the backend cannot.

## A. Payments (the largest change)

| # | Mockup / structure proposal says | Reality | Decision | Spec |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DB-01 | Screen 08 "Payment": opens a hosted UPI gateway, "Opening UPI checkout…", polls `GET /shop/payments/:id/status` every 3 s | No gateway and no `/shop/payments/*` routes. Payment is **peer-to-peer UPI**: the buyer pays the *seller's* UPI ID and submits a 12-digit UTR | *Backend + New*: replace with **Pay the seller** screen (copyable UPI ID, exact amount, order reference, countdown, Open-UPI-app intent, QR, UTR form, optional screenshot, then "waiting for the seller") | 03 |
| DB-02 | `payment_poller.dart`, `payment_repository.dart`, `payment_status.dart`, 2-min poll cap, 8 s gateway timeout, "Payment page didn't open", "Still waiting for confirmation" | None of it exists | *Backend*: drop. Poll **the order** (`GET /shop/orders/:id`) every 15 s only while `awaiting_payment` or `claimed`; refresh when a countdown hits 0 | 01, 03 |
| DB-03 | Stage control "UPI outcome: Confirms / Gateway down / Times out" | Real outcomes: seller confirms, expiry (30 min + 10 min grace), seller "not received" → dispute, buyer dispute after 24 h | *New*: states `awaiting_payment`, `claimed`, `confirmed`, `disputed`, `refund_due`, `refunded`, `void`, `written_off` | 03, 06 |
| DB-04 | Payment screen button "Switch to cash on delivery" | There is no endpoint to change an order's method | *Backend*: remove. Buyer cancels the unpaid order, then re-checks out with COD | 03 |
| DB-05 | `url_launcher` "opens the gateway URL" | There is no gateway URL | *Backend*: `url_launcher` opens the **`upi://pay?...` link** returned in `payee.upi_link` (buyer only, unpaid orders only); copying the UPI ID is the primary path because UPI apps may block links to personal VPAs | 03 |
| DB-06 | UTR is auto-assigned when the gateway confirms; read-only on order detail | The **buyer types** the UTR (spaces ignored, unique platform-wide); the seller must **send back the UTR they verified** to confirm | *Backend*: UTR input (4-4-4 grouping), confirm sheet shows amount + UTR to match in the bank app | 03, 06 |
| DB-07 | Seller order screen starts at `placed` (accept/reject) — copied from the old `ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS` | Fulfilment steps are only allowed once payment is `confirmed` (or COD). A UPI order is first `claimed` and needs **Confirm payment received** / **Not received** | *Backend*: the seller action bar renders from `allowed_actions` (`confirm`, `reject`, `advance:accepted|packed|shipped|delivered`, `refund`, `dispute`) | 03 |
| DB-08 | Status chip map has only the 8 order statuses | Orders have a second axis, `payment_status` (10 values) | *New*: add `PaymentStatusChip` colours/labels (see tokens) and show both chips where relevant | 02, 03 |
| DB-09 | No chat, evidence, disputes, refunds | All exist (per-order chat, private payment screenshots with 10-minute signed URLs, disputes, refund UTR, admin resolution) | *New*: screens designed in the mockup's language | 03 |
| DB-10 | Customer "Cancel order" for `pending_payment` **or** `placed` | A buyer can cancel only while `awaiting_payment`, or a COD order that is still `placed`; after claiming payment they must use chat/dispute | *Backend*: show Cancel only if `allowed_actions` contains `cancel`; cancel dialog adds "Already paid? Submit your UTR instead" | 03 |
| DB-11 | Rejecting a paid order: "arrange the refund with the customer" (copy only) | Reject → `refund_due`; seller must record a **refund UTR** (≠ any payment UTR); buyer can dispute a missing refund | *Backend*: refund form + states | 03, 06 |
| DB-12 | Confirmation "UPI · paid" label | After checkout a UPI order is `awaiting_payment`, not paid | *Backend*: confirmation shows "UPI · pay the seller now" with a Pay button | 03 |
| DB-13 | Home/seller "This week" stat, "To accept" stat | Under P2P the actionable counts are: orders **to confirm payment**, **to ship**, **refunds due** (from `GET /shop/orders/action-count` and `needs_action`) | *Backend*: My-shop stats = Products · To confirm · To ship (derive from the seller order list) | 03 |

## B. Cart, checkout and data shape

| # | Mockup / structure proposal says | Reality | Decision | Spec |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DB-20 | Structure doc: "Cart: server-backed `AsyncNotifier`… rolls back if the request fails" | **No cart API.** (The mockup's own screen is client-side: "Your cart stays on this device.") | *Backend*: local cart per user, persisted, revalidated with `POST /shop/products/lookup`; "Undo" after remove is local | 01, 03, 06 |
| DB-21 | Cart lines `{pid, qty}` with prices from the catalogue | Prices change; stale carts happen | *Backend*: on cart open and before checkout call `lookup`; show price/quantity/availability changes; drop unavailable lines with a notice | 03, 06 |
| DB-22 | Checkout "Place order" splits locally and may place orders one by one | One request: `POST /shop/checkout` with `Idempotency-Key`; backend creates **one order per shop in one transaction** | *Backend*: single call; key kept on retry, rotated when cart/choices change | 03, 06 |
| DB-23 | A single payment method for the whole checkout | Method is **per shop** (`payment_methods` map), UPI only if the shop has a UPI ID (`accepts_upi`) | *Backend*: per-shop method selector; UPI disabled for COD-only shops | 03 |
| DB-24 | "Open UPI" assumed to work for every shop | A shop without a UPI ID is COD-only | *Backend* | 03 |
| DB-25 | Max 3 unpaid UPI orders not mentioned | Backend caps a buyer at 3 open UPI orders (`MAX_OPEN_UPI_ORDERS`) | *Backend*: surface the server message | 06 |
| DB-26 | Multi-order payment "pays one after another" automatically | Backend returns all orders; each must be paid separately | *New*: **Pay your orders** list (`GET /shop/checkouts/:id`) | 03 |

## C. Addresses, shops, products

| # | Mockup says | Reality | Decision | Spec |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DB-30 | Address has `landmark` | Fields are `label, full_name, phone, line1, line2, city, state, pincode, is_default` | *Backend*: the "Landmark" field maps to **`line2`** (label it "Landmark / area (optional)") | 03, 05 |
| DB-31 | Mobile "10 digits"; pincode "6 digits" | Backend only requires non-empty phone (≤20) and numeric pincode 4–10 digits | *Mockup* (stricter, client-side) | 03, 06 |
| DB-32 | Address delete: "Past orders keep their copy" | True after the migration (orders snapshot the address; `address_id` set NULL on delete) | *Mockup* | 03 |
| DB-33 | Seller UPI ID regex `^[\w.-]+@[\w]+$` | Backend: `^[\w.+-]{2,256}@[a-zA-Z]{2,64}$`; blank = COD-only shop | *Backend* regex; helper copy explains COD-only | 03, 06 |
| DB-34 | UPI ID editable freely in Shop profile | Changing it needs `current_password`; recorded in the audit log; open orders keep the old payee | *Backend*: password dialog | 03 |
| DB-35 | Rejected application reason hard-coded | Server returns `rejection_reason` (required for reject **and** suspend; also shown for suspended) | *Backend* | 03 |
| DB-36 | Seller states: none / pending / rejected / suspended / approved from "`/shop/my-shop`" | `GET /shop/shops/mine` → `data.shop` is `null` when none (not 404), else a shop with `status` | *Backend* | 03 |
| DB-37 | Suspended: "Open orders can still be completed" | True: suspended shops see an **orders-only** dashboard; unpaid orders were cancelled and their UPI hidden | *Backend + Mockup* | 03 |
| DB-38 | Seven hard-coded categories with short names and hues | `GET /shop/categories` (admin can add more). Seeded: Devotional Items, Books & Booklets, Apparel & Merchandise, Event Tickets, Handmade & Crafts, Food & Snacks, Other | *Client*: map seeded **slugs** to the mockup's short name + hue (`devotional-items`→Devotional/28°, `books-booklets`→Books/205°, `apparel-merchandise`→Apparel/345°, `event-tickets`→Tickets/265°, `handmade-crafts`→Handmade/150°, `food-snacks`→Food/12°, `other`→Other/45°); for any other category: short = first word, hue = `(id × 47) mod 360` | 02, 03 |
| DB-39 | Product images are initial-letter tiles | Products have `images[]` (Cloudinary URLs, up to 6, `is_primary`) | *Mockup* tile as the **fallback/placeholder**; show real images with `cached_network_image` | 03 |
| DB-40 | Product form: Draft/Active toggle only; category chips | Backend statuses: `draft, active, out_of_stock, archived`; `out_of_stock` is not set automatically; archive = soft remove | *Mockup* + show `Out of stock` chip when `stock_quantity` is 0 | 03 |
| DB-41 | "MRP (₹)" must be higher than price | Backend accepts any `compare_at_price` ≥ 0 | *Mockup* (client-side rule) | 03 |
| DB-42 | Product form posts partial/optional changes freely | `PUT /shop/products/:id` currently requires the **full body** (title, price, stock_quantity) and cannot clear fields | *Backend*: always send the full form; see 12-BACKEND-ASKS (`PATCH`) | 03, 05 |

## D. Search, filters, home

|---|---|---|---|---|
| DB-50 | Search filter: **deanery** chips (Jalandhar/Ludhiana/Amritsar/Kapurthala) on products | `GET /shop/products` supports only `q`, `category_id`, `shop_id`, `page`, `limit`; **shops** (`GET /shop/shops`) support `parish`, `deanery`, `q` | *Client + Backend*: product search uses `q` + `category_id`; the deanery filter lives on a **Shops** result section (`/shop/shops?deanery=`); backlog item in doc 12 | 03, 12 |

| DB-52 | "Featured shops", promo carousel ("5 parish shops just joined", "Advent collection") | No endpoint for featured/promos | *Client*: featured = first shops from `GET /shop/shops?limit=10`; promo cards are static app content in `strings.dart` (flagged as placeholder copy to be replaced by the owner) | 03 |

| DB-55 | "7 categories" label hard-coded | Count from the API | *Backend* | 03 |

| DB-66 | Structure doc: `auth_interceptor` "mirrors `src/api/apiClient.js`" which redirects to `/login` | The web handler leaves the stored `user` behind and routes to a URL that does not exist; the app clears **both** token and user and navigates through the router | 01 |
| DB-67 | Structure doc packages omit sharing/printing/PDF/image resize | Needed for the ID card and uploads | 01 |

## How to use this file
- Implementing a screen: read its spec section **and** the rows that cite it.
- Reviewing: any behaviour that matches the *Mockup / proposal says* column where the decision is *Backend* is a bug.

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/12-BACKEND-ASKS.md ===== -->


# 12 · Backend asks (optional improvements)

The app is built against the backend **as it is** (docs 05/06/08). Nothing here blocks M0–M10. These are changes that would
make the app better, safer or simpler, each with the **workaround the app uses today**, so the owner can schedule backend work
independently. Priority: **P1** = worth doing before public launch, **P2** = soon after, **P3** = nice to have.
Items marked *(verify)* were observed in the code during the kit's preparation; confirm against the backend commit you target.

| # | Ask | Why | App workaround today | Prio |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| B1 | **Refresh tokens / sliding sessions** (or a longer-lived mobile token with server-side revocation) | The JWT lasts 24 h with no refresh; phone users get "Session expired" daily | Re-login prompt; credentials are not stored | P1 |
| B2 | **Logout / token revocation** and "revoke all sessions" after password change | A stolen token stays valid for 24 h | Token deleted locally only | P1 |
| B3 | **Push notifications** (FCM/APNs): device-token registration endpoint + events for the shop (payment claimed, payment confirmed/not received, order shipped, dispute opened/resolved, chat message, shop approved/suspended) | Sellers must confirm payments within 24 h and buyers within 30 min; without push they only see it when they open the app | Action badge from `action-count` polled every 60 s while foregrounded; in-app banners | P1 |
| B4 | **Master-data endpoint** for deaneries/parishes/designations/levels (`GET /meta/diocese`) | The lists are constants inside the web form; the app bundles a copy (`assets/data/deaneries.json`, with known quirks) that will drift | Bundled JSON + `GET /profiles/filter-options` for filters | P1 |
| B5 | **Make `date_of_birth` consistently required** (validator says optional, create returns 500 without it) — or make it genuinely optional | A 500 on a user error | App requires it in the form | P1 *(verify)* |
| B6 | **Consistent field names** between write and read for profiles (`father_name`/`father`, `mother_name`/`mother`, `date_of_birth`/`dob`) — accept both on write, or return both on read | Easy source of bugs | Mapping inside the Profile model only | P2 |
| B7 | **Deleted-profiles view and an explicit restore endpoint** (`GET /profiles?deleted=true`, `POST /profiles/:id/restore`) | Today restoring means re-creating a profile with the same normalised name + phone, which silently issues new credentials | Notice in the create dialog; no deleted view | P2 |
| B8 | **Stats that exclude soft-deleted profiles** (or return both counts) | `GET /profiles/stats` counts deleted rows *(verify)* | Shown as returned with doc 04 wording | P2 |
| B9 | **Enforce photo limits and type server-side with clear messages** (≤5 MB, JPEG/PNG/WebP, minimum dimensions) | Only a body-size limit exists on the profile photo path *(verify)* | Client resizes to ≤1600 px / ≤1 MB | P2 |
| B10 | **Password policy parity**: the same complexity rules on `/auth/change-password` and `/profile-holder/change-password` (and on registration) | Rules differ per endpoint *(verify)* | App applies the stricter set to both | P2 |
| B11 | **Holder first-login password change** (force change of the phone-number initial password) | Initial password equals the phone number, which is guessable | Not enforced; copy in the credentials dialog advises changing it | P1 |
| B12 | **Pagination metadata on every list** (`page`, `limit`, `total`, `has_next`) and the same query names everywhere | Some lists return less metadata than others | App infers `hasNext` from page size where metadata is missing | P3 |
| B13 | **Richer product listing**: `sort` (price, newest), `min_price`/`max_price`, `in_stock`, `deanery` | `/shop/products` supports only `q`, `category_id`, `shop_id`, `page`, `limit` | Client-side sort/filter on the loaded page (incomplete across pages) | P2 |
| B14 | **Server-side cart** (optional) | Client cart cannot sync across devices | Local cart per user with lookup revalidation | P3 |
| B15 | **Order-total preview endpoint** (shipping, per-shop split) before checkout | Totals are computed at checkout; the review step shows client estimates | Estimate from `lookup` data, final amounts from the checkout response | P3 |
| B16 | **Seller UPI ID verification** (e.g. penny-drop / name match via a licensed provider) | A wrong UPI ID sends money to the wrong person; the platform cannot prevent it | Strong warning copy + copy-and-verify UI on the seller's profile and pay screen | P2 |
| B17 | **Account deletion / data export endpoint** | Needed for store policies and privacy law expectations | Deletion by contacting the CYD Office (doc 13 §5) | P1 |
| B18 | **Audit log of admin actions** (dispute resolutions, shop suspensions, profile deletes) | Accountability for a moderation role | None | P2 |
| B19 | **Rate-limit headers** (`Retry-After`, `RateLimit-*`) on 429 | The app shows fixed 15-minute copy | Fixed copy from doc 04 | P3 |
| B20 | **API versioning prefix** (`/api/v1`) and an OpenAPI document generated from the code | Lets the app and docs evolve safely; the contract verifier could diff mechanically | Doc 05 maintained by hand | P2 |
| B21 | **Backend `GET /health` with a version/commit field** | The app and QA could show/verify the backend build in use | Plain `/health` only | P3 |
| B22 | **Image delivery**: Cloudinary transformations in returned URLs (thumbnails) or a `thumb_url` field | Lists load full-size images on mobile data | `cached_network_image` with `memCacheWidth`; URL transformation by string insertion if the URL is a Cloudinary one | P2 |
| B24 | **Password reset / credential re-issue** for profile holders (admin-triggered, returns a new one-time password) | The holder's credentials are returned only once at creation; a forgotten password has no recovery path in the API | Admin deletes and re-creates the profile (restore-by-recreation issues new credentials) — see doc 04 | P1 |

## How to use this list
1. Decide with the CYD Office which P1 items must precede store submission (B1, B3, B4, B5, B11, B17 are the usual launch blockers or near-blockers).
2. When a backend item ships, the **api-contract-verifier** will report the doc/app drift; update `docs/05`, `docs/08`, remove the workaround, and note the change in `docs/11-DESIGN-VS-BACKEND.md`.
3. Never rely on an unshipped ask in app code. Features that depend on one (push, refresh) are built behind an interface with a no-op implementation, so shipping the backend side later needs no redesign.

<!-- ===== FILE: docs/13-RELEASE.md ===== -->


# 13 · Release: identifiers, signing, privacy, stores, CI/CD

> Owned by the **release-engineer** agent (M10). Items marked **OWNER** need a decision or an asset only the
> owner can provide. Nothing here is legal advice: have the diocese's advisers confirm privacy and payment wording.

## 1. Identity (OWNER decisions, then never change)

| Item | Proposal | Status |
|---|---|---|
| App name (store) | "CYD" (subtitle: "Catholic Yuva Dhara, Jalandhar") | OWNER to confirm |
| Android `applicationId` / iOS bundle id | `<reverse-domain-of-the-organisation>.cyd` (cannot be changed after the first store release) | **OWNER** |
| Publisher account | Diocese/CYD organisation account (Google Play Console, Apple Developer) rather than a personal account | **OWNER** |
| Icon / splash | Need a **standalone logo**: the repos contain none (the emblems exist only inside the card backgrounds). Provide a 1024×1024 PNG with safe area for adaptive icons | **OWNER / designer** |
| Support contact | A monitored email + the CYD Office address, shown in the store listing and in-app (a "Help & support" row to be added to the Account screen — **Designed here**, owner approval needed) | **OWNER** |
| Versioning | `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH+BUILD` in `pubspec.yaml`; build number increments on every upload | agent |

## 2. Configuration and flavors
- `--dart-define=API_BASE_URL=https://cyd-id-be.onrender.com` for production (or the owner's own domain when the backend moves). `FLAVOR=prod`.
- Release builds must **not** contain cleartext exceptions; keep them in `android/app/src/debug/AndroidManifest.xml` and a debug-only iOS configuration.
- Render's free tier sleeps: for launch, recommend a paid always-on instance or a keep-alive, otherwise first requests take 30–60 s.

## 3. Android
- Min SDK per Flutter default (or 26); target SDK = the current Play requirement at submission time (check Play Console).
- Permissions (keep minimal): `INTERNET`; `CAMERA` only if the picker uses it directly (prefer the system picker/intent, which needs none); media access for **saving** the card — use the scoped-storage/MediaStore path (no broad storage permissions).
- `<queries>` entry for the `upi` scheme so `url_launcher` can detect UPI apps (Android 11+).
- Signing: create an upload keystore (kept out of git, backed up in two places), enable **Play App Signing**; `key.properties` ignored by git; CI uses encrypted secrets.
- Build: `flutter build appbundle --release --dart-define=...`; enable R8/shrink; verify ProGuard keeps nothing app-specific.
- Test on at least: a low-end 2 GB device, a mid-range, and an Android 14+ device.

## 4. iOS
- `Info.plist` usage strings: camera, photo library read (picker) and add-only (save card), with plain-language reasons.
- `LSApplicationQueriesSchemes` entry for `upi` (and common UPI apps' schemes if detection is needed).
- App Transport Security: HTTPS only in release.
- Signing/provisioning with the organisation's Apple Developer account; TestFlight internal testing first.
- Review notes for App Review: explain the three roles, provide **test accounts for each role** (admin, manager, holder, buyer, seller) on a staging backend with sample data, and explain that payments happen peer-to-peer outside the app (no in-app purchase): the app lists physical goods/event tickets from parish shops and displays the seller's UPI ID.

## 5. Privacy and data safety (draft answers — review with advisers)

What the app handles:
- **Account**: username, email (generated or admin-entered), role, hashed password (server-side).
- **Profiles (youth members, may include minors)**: name, parents' names, date of birth, baptism date, phone, postal address, parish/deanery, qualification, photo, issue date.
- **Shop**: delivery addresses (name, phone, address), orders, chat messages, **payment screenshots** (may reveal bank details), UTRs, sellers' UPI IDs.
- **Device**: nothing beyond what the OS exposes to normal apps; no advertising ID, no analytics SDK, no location.

Draft store answers: data is collected and linked to the user; used for app functionality only; not sold; not used for advertising; encrypted in transit (HTTPS); users can request deletion by contacting the CYD Office (profile deletion is an admin action; accounts are created by staff, not self-registered). **OWNER** to confirm retention periods (e.g. soft-deleted profiles keep their data) and to publish a privacy policy URL covering the above, including handling of minors' data and parental consent as the diocese requires. India's **Digital Personal Data Protection Act, 2023** may apply to this processing — get advice.

In-app: show a short privacy notice at first login (link to the policy), explain why the photo and phone are collected, and never log or screenshot-prompt sensitive data.

## 6. Payments and marketplace policy notes
- The platform **never receives or holds money**: buyers pay sellers directly by UPI. The app only displays a UPI ID, opens a `upi://` link/QR, and records the buyer's reference number. This is a deliberately lighter position than aggregating funds, but check Google Play's payments/financial-features declarations and Apple's guidelines for apps that link to payments, and describe this accurately in the listing and review notes.
- Disputes: the admin's real lever is suspending a shop and following up through the parish; say so plainly in the in-app help text (do not promise buyer protection the platform cannot deliver).
- Content rules for the marketplace (what may be sold) are a diocesan decision; add a reporting contact and take-down process before launch (**OWNER**).

## 7. Store assets and listing
- Screenshots (phone 6.7" and 5.5", plus tablet if supported) from seeded data: My ID card, Profiles list, Shop home, Product, Pay the seller, Order detail, Seller dashboard — **no real personal data or real photos**.
- Short and full descriptions (English), category (Lifestyle/Shopping or Social), content rating questionnaire answers, privacy policy URL, support email, website.
- Feature graphic (Play) and promotional text (App Store) in the app's saffron/black language (`design/tokens.json`).

## 8. CI/CD
- **On every PR**: `flutter pub get`, `dart format --set-exit-if-changed`, `flutter analyze`, `flutter test` (unit + widget + golden).
- **On tag `v*`**: build signed AAB (secrets: keystore base64, passwords) → upload as artifact (optionally to Play internal track with a service account); iOS via Xcode Cloud/fastlane when the Apple account exists.
- Cache pub and Gradle; pin the Flutter version in CI (`.fvmrc` or workflow) so builds are reproducible.
- Integration tests run manually or on a self-hosted emulator job against the local-backend docker/compose if the owner adds one (not required).

## 9. Release checklist
- [ ] Owner decisions: names, ids, accounts, logo, support contact, privacy policy URL, retention.
- [ ] Version/build bumped; changelog written.
- [ ] Release build uses HTTPS prod URL; no debug banner; no cleartext; no logs of sensitive data.
- [ ] Smoke test on a clean device for every role (the per-role checklists in docs/10 §5).
- [ ] Contract verifier clean against the **merged** production backend commit; seed/staging data cleaned.
- [ ] Store listings, screenshots, privacy/data-safety forms, content rating completed.
- [ ] Internal testing → closed testing with the CYD Office team → staged production rollout (5% → 20% → 100%) with a rollback plan (previous AAB retained).
- [ ] Post-release: monitor store reviews and support mailbox; plan the first update (notifications, backend asks in doc 12).
